chi_adm_imperial_law_dispatch = {
 if = {
  limit = {
   type = law_type:law_local_police
  }
  owner = {
   every_subject_or_below = {
    limit = {
     chi_adm_imperial_law_civil = yes
     NOT = {
      has_law = law_type:law_local_police
     }
     NOT = {
      exists = imposed_law
     }
     NOT = {
      has_journal_entry = je_law_imposition
     }
    }
    chi_adm_imperial_law_receive = {
     ID = 1
    }
   }
  }
 }

chi_adm_imperial_law_stop_support = {
 if = {
  limit = {
   has_variable = chi_adm_imperial_law_support
  }
  remove_enactment_modifier = chi_adm_imperial_law_enactment_support
  remove_variable = chi_adm_imperial_law_support
 }
 remove_variable = chi_adm_imperial_law_support_left
 remove_modifier = chi_adm_imperial_law_implementation_lock
 remove_variable = chi_adm_imperial_law_implementation_wait
 remove_variable = chi_adm_imperial_law_implementation_paused
 remove_variable = chi_adm_imperial_law_resumed_clock
}
chi_adm_imperial_law_clear = {
 remove_variable = chi_adm_imperial_law_rejection_final
 remove_variable = chi_adm_imperial_law_extra_delay_used
 remove_variable = chi_adm_imperial_law_delay_decision
 chi_adm_imperial_law_stop_support = yes
 remove_variable = chi_adm_imperial_law_active
 remove_variable = chi_adm_imperial_law_law_id
 remove_variable = chi_adm_imperial_law_status
 remove_variable = chi_adm_imperial_law_months_left
 remove_variable = chi_adm_imperial_law_delay_used
 remove_variable = chi_adm_imperial_law_petition_used
 remove_variable = chi_adm_imperial_law_refusal_used
 remove_variable = chi_adm_imperial_law_court_answered
 remove_variable = chi_adm_imperial_law_support_used
 remove_variable = chi_adm_imperial_law_backlog_id
 remove_variable = chi_adm_imperial_law_law_group
 remove_variable = chi_adm_imperial_law_incoming_group
}
chi_adm_imperial_law_receive = {
 chi_adm_imperial_law_set_current_group = yes
 chi_adm_imperial_law_set_incoming_group = {
  ID = $ID$
 }
 if = {
  limit = {
   chi_adm_imperial_law_civil = yes
   NOT = { has_variable = chi_adm_imperial_law_last_rejected_id var:chi_adm_imperial_law_last_rejected_id = $ID$ }
   NOT = {
    has_variable = chi_adm_imperial_law_repeat_$ID$
   }
   NOT = {
    AND = {
     has_variable = chi_adm_imperial_law_active
     var:chi_adm_imperial_law_law_id = $ID$
    }
   }
  }
  if = {
   limit = {
    has_variable = chi_adm_imperial_law_active
    OR = {
     var:chi_adm_imperial_law_status = 2
     var:chi_adm_imperial_law_status = 3
    }
    NOT = {
     var:chi_adm_imperial_law_law_group = var:chi_adm_imperial_law_incoming_group
    }
   }
   set_variable = {
    name = chi_adm_imperial_law_backlog_id
    value = $ID$
   }
  }
  else = {
   chi_adm_imperial_law_clear = yes
   set_variable = {
    name = chi_adm_imperial_law_law_id
    value = $ID$
   }
   chi_adm_imperial_law_set_current_group = yes
   if = {
    limit = {
     chi_adm_imperial_law_court_still_requests = yes
     chi_adm_imperial_law_adopted = no
    }
    set_variable = chi_adm_imperial_law_active
    set_variable = {
     name = chi_adm_imperial_law_status
     value = 1
    }
    set_variable = {
     name = chi_adm_imperial_law_months_left
     value = chi_adm_imperial_law_response_months
    }
    set_variable = {
     name = chi_adm_imperial_law_repeat_$ID$
     days = chi_adm_imperial_law_repeat_days
    }
    if = {
     limit = {
      NOT = {
       has_journal_entry = chi_adm_imperial_law_directive
      }
     }
     add_journal_entry = {
      type = chi_adm_imperial_law_directive
     }
    }
    trigger_event = {
     id = chi_adm_imperial_law_law.1
     popup = yes
    }
   }
   else = {
    chi_adm_imperial_law_clear = yes
   }
  }
 }
}
chi_adm_imperial_law_bind = {
 every_law = {
  limit = {
   chi_adm_imperial_law_matches_request = yes
  }
  save_scope_as = chi_adm_imperial_law_target_law
 }
}
chi_adm_imperial_law_complete = {
 if = {
  limit = {
   has_variable = chi_adm_imperial_law_active
   chi_adm_imperial_law_adopted = yes
  }
  chi_adm_imperial_law_stop_support = yes
  chi_adm_adjust_loyalty = {
   AMOUNT = chi_adm_imperial_law_loyalty_reward
  }
  chi_adm_adjust_autonomy = {
   AMOUNT = chi_adm_imperial_law_autonomy_reward
  }
  set_variable = {
   name = chi_adm_imperial_law_status
   value = 10
  }
  remove_variable = chi_adm_imperial_law_active
  remove_variable = chi_adm_imperial_law_months_left
  chi_adm_imperial_law_advance_backlog = yes
 }
}
chi_adm_imperial_law_start = {
 if = {
  limit = {
   chi_adm_imperial_law_valid = yes
   enacting_any_law = no
   chi_adm_imperial_law_available = yes
   NOT = {
    var:chi_adm_imperial_law_status = 3
   }
  }
  chi_adm_imperial_law_stop_support = yes
  set_variable = {
   name = chi_adm_imperial_law_status
   value = 3
  }
  set_variable = {
   name = chi_adm_imperial_law_months_left
   value = 12
  }
  set_variable = {
   name = chi_adm_imperial_law_implementation_wait
   days = 360
  }
  add_modifier = {
   name = chi_adm_imperial_law_implementation_lock
  }
  chi_adm_imperial_law_schedule_implementation = yes
 }
 else_if = {
  limit = {
   NOT = {
    var:chi_adm_imperial_law_status = 3
   }
  }
  set_variable = {
   name = chi_adm_imperial_law_status
   value = 8
  }
  trigger_event = {
   id = chi_adm_imperial_law_law.1
   popup = yes
  }
 }
}
chi_adm_imperial_law_immediate = {
 if = {
  limit = {
   chi_adm_imperial_law_can_answer = yes
   enacting_any_law = no
   chi_adm_imperial_law_available = yes
  }
  chi_adm_imperial_law_bind = yes
  activate_law = scope:chi_adm_imperial_law_target_law.type
  add_modifier = {
   name = chi_adm_imperial_law_transition
   months = 12
  }
  chi_adm_imperial_law_complete = yes
 }
}
chi_adm_imperial_law_queue = {
 if = {
  limit = {
   chi_adm_imperial_law_can_answer = yes
   enacting_any_law = yes
  }
  set_variable = {
   name = chi_adm_imperial_law_status
   value = 2
  }
  remove_variable = chi_adm_imperial_law_months_left
 }
}
chi_adm_imperial_law_prioritize = {
 if = {
  limit = {
   chi_adm_imperial_law_can_answer = yes
   enacting_any_law = yes
   chi_adm_imperial_law_available = yes
   modifier:country_cannot_cancel_law_enactment_bool = no
  }
  cancel_enactment = yes
  chi_adm_imperial_law_start = yes
 }
}
chi_adm_imperial_law_delay = {
 if = {
  limit = {
   chi_adm_imperial_law_can_answer = yes
   enacting_any_law = no
   NOT = {
    has_variable = chi_adm_imperial_law_delay_used
   }
  }
  set_variable = chi_adm_imperial_law_delay_used
  set_variable = {
   name = chi_adm_imperial_law_status
   value = 5
  }
  set_variable = {
   name = chi_adm_imperial_law_months_left
   value = chi_adm_imperial_law_response_months
  }
  chi_adm_adjust_loyalty = {
   AMOUNT = chi_adm_imperial_law_delay_loss
  }
 }
}
chi_adm_imperial_law_report = {
 save_scope_as = chi_adm_imperial_law_province
 var:chi_adm_imperial_court ?= {
  if = {
   limit = { is_ai = yes }
   trigger_event = { id = chi_adm_imperial_law_law.2 popup = yes }
  }
 }
}
chi_adm_imperial_law_petition = {
 if = {
  limit = {
   chi_adm_imperial_law_can_answer = yes
   NOT = {
    has_variable = chi_adm_imperial_law_petition_used
   }
  }
  set_variable = chi_adm_imperial_law_petition_used
  remove_variable = chi_adm_imperial_law_court_answered
  set_variable = {
   name = chi_adm_imperial_law_status
   value = 4
  }
  set_variable = {
   name = chi_adm_imperial_law_months_left
   value = chi_adm_imperial_law_response_months
  }
  chi_adm_imperial_law_report = yes
 }

chi_adm_imperial_law_refuse = {
 chi_adm_imperial_law_final_rejection = yes
}
# Pausing keeps the review counter. After resumption the monthly clock is authoritative,
# so a completion event queued before the pause cannot enact the law early.
chi_adm_imperial_law_pause_implementation = {
 if = {
  limit = {
   chi_adm_imperial_law_can_manage_implementation = yes
   NOT = { has_variable = chi_adm_imperial_law_implementation_paused }
  }
  set_variable = chi_adm_imperial_law_implementation_paused
  set_variable = chi_adm_imperial_law_resumed_clock
  remove_variable = chi_adm_imperial_law_implementation_wait
  remove_modifier = chi_adm_imperial_law_implementation_lock
 }
}

chi_adm_imperial_law_resume_implementation = {
 if = {
  limit = {
   chi_adm_imperial_law_can_manage_implementation = yes
   has_variable = chi_adm_imperial_law_implementation_paused
   enacting_any_law = no
   chi_adm_imperial_law_available = yes
  }
  remove_variable = chi_adm_imperial_law_implementation_paused
  add_modifier = { name = chi_adm_imperial_law_implementation_lock }
 }
}

chi_adm_imperial_law_monthly = {
 if = {
  limit = { has_variable = chi_adm_imperial_law_refusal_used OR = { var:chi_adm_imperial_law_status = 1 var:chi_adm_imperial_law_status = 7 } }
  chi_adm_imperial_law_final_rejection = yes
 }
 if = {
  limit = {
   chi_adm_imperial_law_civil = no
  }
  chi_adm_imperial_law_clear = yes
 }
 else_if = {
  limit = {
   OR = {
    chi_adm_imperial_law_court_still_requests = no
    exists = imposed_law
    has_journal_entry = je_law_imposition
   }
  }
  chi_adm_imperial_law_stop_support = yes
  set_variable = {
   name = chi_adm_imperial_law_status
   value = 11
  }
  remove_variable = chi_adm_imperial_law_active
  chi_adm_imperial_law_advance_backlog = yes
 }
 else_if = {
  limit = {
   chi_adm_imperial_law_adopted = yes
  }
  chi_adm_imperial_law_complete = yes
 }
 else = {
  if = {
   limit = {
    has_variable = chi_adm_imperial_law_support
   }
   chi_adm_imperial_law_stop_support = yes
  }
  if = {
   limit = {
    var:chi_adm_imperial_law_status = 3
    NOT = { has_variable = chi_adm_imperial_law_implementation_paused }
    NOT = {
     has_modifier = chi_adm_imperial_law_implementation_lock
    }
   }
   if = {
    limit = {
     enacting_any_law = yes
    }
    set_variable = {
     name = chi_adm_imperial_law_status
     value = 2
    }
   }
   else = {
    set_variable = {
     name = chi_adm_imperial_law_status
     value = 1
    }
    chi_adm_imperial_law_start = yes
   }
  }
  if = {
   limit = {
    var:chi_adm_imperial_law_status = 3
    NOT = { has_variable = chi_adm_imperial_law_implementation_paused }
    has_variable = chi_adm_imperial_law_months_left
   }
   if = {
    limit = {
     var:chi_adm_imperial_law_months_left > 0
    }
    change_variable = {
     name = chi_adm_imperial_law_months_left
     subtract = 1
    }
   }
   if = {
    limit = {
     var:chi_adm_imperial_law_months_left <= 0
    }
    remove_variable = chi_adm_imperial_law_implementation_wait
    chi_adm_imperial_law_finish_implementation = yes
   }
  }
  if = {
   limit = {
    chi_adm_imperial_law_court_case = yes
    var:chi_adm_imperial_court = {
     is_ai = yes
    }
   }
   if = {
    limit = {
     var:chi_adm_loyalty >= 60
     OR = {
      var:chi_adm_autonomy >= 40
      chi_adm_imperial_law_available = no
     }
    }
    chi_adm_imperial_law_court_grant = yes
   }
   else_if = {
    limit = {
     chi_adm_imperial_law_available = yes
    }
    chi_adm_imperial_law_court_offer = yes
   }
   else = {
    chi_adm_imperial_law_court_maintain = yes
   }
  }
  if = {
   limit = {
    var:chi_adm_imperial_law_status = 2
    enacting_any_law = no
   }
   chi_adm_imperial_law_start = yes
  }
  if = {
   limit = {
    has_variable = chi_adm_imperial_law_months_left
    NOT = {
     var:chi_adm_imperial_law_status = 3
    }
   }
   change_variable = {
    name = chi_adm_imperial_law_months_left
    subtract = 1
   }
   if = {
    limit = {
     var:chi_adm_imperial_law_months_left <= 0
    }
    if = {
     limit = {
      var:chi_adm_imperial_law_status = 4
     }
     set_variable = chi_adm_imperial_law_court_answered
    }
    if = {
     limit = { var:chi_adm_imperial_law_status = 5 }
     set_variable = chi_adm_imperial_law_delay_decision
    }
    if = {
     limit = {
      var:chi_adm_imperial_law_status = 7
     }
     set_variable = chi_adm_imperial_law_court_answered
    }
    else = {
     set_variable = {
      name = chi_adm_imperial_law_status
      value = 9
     }
    }
    remove_variable = chi_adm_imperial_law_months_left
    if = {
     limit = { has_variable = chi_adm_imperial_law_delay_decision }
     trigger_event = { id = chi_adm_imperial_law_law.91 popup = yes }
    }
    else = { trigger_event = { id = chi_adm_imperial_law_law.1 popup = yes } }
   }
  }
 }
}
chi_adm_imperial_law_court_grant = {
 if = {
  limit = {
   chi_adm_imperial_law_court_case = yes
   var:chi_adm_imperial_court = {
    NOT = {
     has_modifier = chi_adm_imperial_law_court_workload
    }
    authority >= 25
   }
  }
  set_variable = chi_adm_imperial_law_court_answered
  set_variable = {
   name = chi_adm_imperial_law_status
   value = 6
  }
  set_variable = {
   name = chi_adm_imperial_law_months_left
   value = chi_adm_imperial_law_exemption_months
  }
  chi_adm_adjust_autonomy = {
   AMOUNT = chi_adm_imperial_law_refusal_autonomy
  }
  var:chi_adm_imperial_court = {
   add_modifier = {
    name = chi_adm_imperial_law_court_workload
    months = 6
   }
  }
  trigger_event = {
   id = chi_adm_imperial_law_law.1
   popup = yes
  }
 }
}
chi_adm_imperial_law_court_offer = {
 if = {
  limit = { has_variable = chi_adm_imperial_law_refusal_used }
  chi_adm_imperial_law_court_maintain = yes
 }
 if = {
  limit = {
   NOT = { has_variable = chi_adm_imperial_law_refusal_used }
   chi_adm_imperial_law_court_case = yes
   var:chi_adm_imperial_court = {
    NOT = {
     has_modifier = chi_adm_imperial_law_court_workload
    }
    authority >= 25
   }
  }
  set_variable = chi_adm_imperial_law_court_answered
  set_variable = {
   name = chi_adm_imperial_law_status
   value = 1
  }
  set_variable = {
   name = chi_adm_imperial_law_months_left
   value = chi_adm_imperial_law_response_months
  }
  var:chi_adm_imperial_court = {
   add_modifier = {
    name = chi_adm_imperial_law_court_workload
    months = 6
   }
  }
  trigger_event = {
   id = chi_adm_imperial_law_law.1
   popup = yes
  }
 }
}
chi_adm_imperial_law_court_maintain = {
 if = {
  limit = {
   chi_adm_imperial_law_court_case = yes
  }
  set_variable = chi_adm_imperial_law_court_answered
  if = {
   limit = {
    var:chi_adm_imperial_law_status = 7
   }
   chi_adm_adjust_loyalty = {
    AMOUNT = chi_adm_imperial_law_delay_loss
   }
   change_relations = {
    country = var:chi_adm_imperial_court
    value = -10
   }
   remove_variable = chi_adm_imperial_law_months_left
  }
  else = {
   set_variable = {
    name = chi_adm_imperial_law_status
    value = 1
   }
   set_variable = {
    name = chi_adm_imperial_law_months_left
    value = chi_adm_imperial_law_response_months
   }
  }
  trigger_event = {
   id = chi_adm_imperial_law_law.1
   popup = yes
  }
 }
}

}
chi_adm_imperial_law_finish_implementation = {
 if = {
  limit = {
   chi_adm_imperial_law_valid = yes
   var:chi_adm_imperial_law_status = 3
   NOR = {
    has_variable = chi_adm_imperial_law_implementation_wait
    has_variable = chi_adm_imperial_law_implementation_paused
   }
   trigger_if = {
    limit = { has_variable = chi_adm_imperial_law_resumed_clock }
    var:chi_adm_imperial_law_months_left <= 0
   }
  }
  remove_modifier = chi_adm_imperial_law_implementation_lock
  if = {
   limit = {
    chi_adm_imperial_law_available = yes
   }
   chi_adm_imperial_law_bind = yes
   activate_law = scope:chi_adm_imperial_law_target_law.type
   chi_adm_imperial_law_complete = yes
  }
  else = {
   set_variable = {
    name = chi_adm_imperial_law_status
    value = 8
   }
   remove_variable = chi_adm_imperial_law_months_left
   trigger_event = {
    id = chi_adm_imperial_law_law.1
    popup = yes
   }
  }
 }
